fn verdict_of(word: &str) -> char {
    let toks: Vec<CTok> = word.chars().filter_map(|c| CTok::parse(&c.to_string())).collect();
    check::word_verdict(&toks).0
}

/// The four calibration words. Cheap: none exceeds a few dozen tokens.
pub fn report() -> String {
    let mut s = String::new();
    s.push_str("=== PROOFLIFT — proof terms judged by the on-board kernel ===\n\n");
    s.push_str("A proof term is a graph. A case analysis is the fork, the branches\n");
    s.push_str("landing on one goal is the fuse, an undischarged claim is a fork with\n");
    s.push_str("nothing to pair it. No rule was added for proofs; these are the verdicts\n");
    s.push_str("this kernel already returns for compiled control flow.\n\n");
